The (aux machine) modules

Environments, the SC machine and the SECD machine

 Posted on October 21, 2026  |   271 min  |   Massimo Nocentini

(aux machine env): environments for abstract machines

Tabled environments

Ported from on-scheme's commons.scm; the checks come from SC-machine-test.scm.

Implementation

(module
  (aux machine env)
  *
  (import
    scheme
    (chicken base)
    (chicken condition)
    (only (chicken memory representation) extend-procedure procedure-data)
    srfi-69
    (aux base)
    (aux commons)
    (aux tabling))
  (define extend
    (λ (E #!key (same? equal?))
       (let* ((lookup
                (λ (p E z)
                   (letcar&cdr
                     (((x y) p))
                     (cond ((same? x z) y) (else (E z))))))
              (extend₁
                (cond ((and (eq? same? equal?)
                            (not (eq? (procedure-data E) 'untabled)))
                       (λ (p E)
                          (letrec-tabled
                            ((E₁ H
                                 (lambda (z)
                                   (cond ((eq? tabled/get-hidden-hash-table z)
                                          (hash-table-merge
                                            (H)
                                            (E z #:fresh #t #:store #f)))
                                         (else (lookup p E z))))))
                            E₁)))
                      (else
                       (λ (p E)
                          (extend-procedure
                            (λ (z #!key (fresh (void)) (store (void)))
                               (cond ((eq? tabled/get-hidden-hash-table z)
                                      (E z #:fresh #t #:store #f))
                                     (else (lookup p E z))))
                            'untabled))))))
         (λ assocs (foldr extend₁ E assocs)))))
  (define E₀
    (λ (z #!key (fresh (void)) (store (void)))
       (cond ((eq? tabled/get-hidden-hash-table z) (make-hash-table))
             (else (void)))))
  (define E⁺
    (λ (z #!key (fresh (void)) (store (void)))
       (cond ((eq? tabled/get-hidden-hash-table z) (make-hash-table))
             ((atom? z) (handle-exceptions _ (void) (eval z)))
             (else (void)))))
  (define E->alist
    (λ (E)
       (hash-table-fold
         (E tabled/get-hidden-hash-table #:fresh #t #:store #f)
         (λ (k v acc) (cons `(,k unquote v) acc))
         '())))
  (define E-null? (=to? E₀ #:same? eq?)))

test/extend-lookup: pass

(define (test/extend-lookup _)
  (define E₁ ((extend E₀) '(a . 3)))
  (define E₂ ((extend E₁) '(b . 4)))
  (⊦= 3 (E₂ 'a))
  (⊦= 4 (E₂ 'b))
  (⊨ (void? (E₂ 'c)))
  (⊦= 3 (E₂ 'a))
  (⊦=/alist `(((c) unquote (void)) ((a) . 3) ((b) . 4)) (E->alist E₂))
  (define E₃ ((extend E₂) '(b . 0)))
  (⊦=/alist `(((c) unquote (void)) ((a) . 3) ((b) . 4)) (E->alist E₃))
  (⊦= 0 (E₃ 'b))
  (⊦=/alist `(((c) unquote (void)) ((a) . 3) ((b) . 0)) (E->alist E₃)))

test/E-null?: pass

(define (test/E-null? _)
  (define E₁ ((extend E₀) '(a . 3)))
  (⊨ (E-null? E₀))
  (⊭ (E-null? E₁)))

test/E⁺: pass

(define (test/E⁺ _)
  (define E ((extend E⁺) '(a . 3)))
  (⊦= 3 (E 'a))
  (⊦= 7 (E 7))
  (⊦= car (E 'car))
  (⊨ (void? (E 'an-unbound-identifier)))
  (⊨ (void? (E '(car x)))))

test/extend-many: pass

(define (test/extend-many _)
  (define E ((extend E₀) '(a . 1) '(b . 2) '(a . 3)))
  (⊦= 1 (E 'a))
  (⊦= 2 (E 'b))
  (define E/eq ((extend E₀ #:same? eq?) (cons "s" 1)))
  (⊨ (void? (E/eq (string #\s))))
  (define E/equal ((extend E₀) (cons "s" 1)))
  (⊦= 1 (E/equal (string #\s))))

test/extend-same?-untabled: pass

(define (test/extend-same?-untabled _)
  (define s "s")
  (define E/miss-first ((extend E₀ #:same? eq?) (cons s 1)))
  (⊨ (void? (E/miss-first (string #\s))))
  (⊦= 1 (E/miss-first s))
  (define E/hit-first ((extend E₀ #:same? eq?) (cons s 1)))
  (⊦= 1 (E/hit-first s))
  (⊨ (void? (E/hit-first (string #\s))))
  (define E/child ((extend ((extend E₀ #:same? eq?) (cons s 1))) '(a . 2)))
  (⊨ (void? (E/child (string #\s))))
  (⊦= 1 (E/child s))
  (⊦= 2 (E/child 'a))
  (⊦=/alist '() (E->alist E/child))
  (define E/tabled ((extend E₀) '(b . 3)))
  (define E/over ((extend E/tabled #:same? eq?) (cons s 1)))
  (⊦= 3 (E/over 'b))
  (⊦=/alist '(((b) . 3)) (E->alist E/over)))

(aux machine sc): the SC machine

The SC machine

Ported from on-scheme's SC-machine.scm.

Implementation

(module
  (aux machine sc)
  *
  (import
    scheme
    (chicken base)
    (chicken format)
    (aux base)
    (aux commons)
    (aux machine env))
  (define %check
    (λ (who pred? v)
       (cond ((pred? v) v) (else (error who "wrong field type" v)))))
  (define-record %Id identifier)
  (define-record %Comb rator rand)
  (define Id (λ (identifier) (make-%Id (%check 'Id symbol? identifier))))
  (define Id? %Id?)
  (define Id-identifier %Id-identifier)
  (define combination? (λ (c) (or (%Id? c) (%Comb? c))))
  (define Comb
    (λ (rator rand)
       (make-%Comb
         (%check 'Comb combination? rator)
         (%check 'Comb combination? rand))))
  (define Comb? %Comb?)
  (define Comb-rator %Comb-rator)
  (define Comb-rand %Comb-rand)
  (set-record-printer! %Id (λ (c out) (format out "~a" (%Id-identifier c))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Comb
    (λ (c out) (format out "(~a ~a)" (%Comb-rator c) (%Comb-rand c))))
  (define curryfy
    (let1 (C (λ (c) (cond ((list? c) (curryfy c)) (else (Id c)))))
          (foldl1 (λ (acc c) (Comb acc (C c))) #:H₀ C)))
  (define value
    (λ (E)
       (rec V
            (λ (c)
               (cond ((%Id? c) (E (%Id-identifier c)))
                     ((%Comb? c) ((V (%Comb-rator c)) (V (%Comb-rand c))))
                     (else (error 'value "not a combination" c)))))))
  (define-record status S C)
  (define dbind/status (λ (recv) (λ (s) (recv s (status-S s) (status-C s)))))
  (set-record-printer!
    status
    (λ (s out)
       (let1 (P (dbind/status (λ (s S C) (format out "(~a ~a)" S C)))) (P s))))
  (define →/interpreted
    (let* ((sym/apply (gensym 'apply)) (is-apply? (=to? sym/apply)))
      (λ (E)
         (dbind/status
           (λ (s S C)
              (cond ((null? C) s)
                    (else
                     (match1/first
                       ((,C₀ unquote C₊) C)
                       (cond ((is-apply? C₀)
                              (match1/first
                                ((,f ,y unquote S₊) S)
                                (make-status (cons (f y) S₊) C₊)))
                             ((%Id? C₀)
                              (let1 (stack (cons (E (%Id-identifier C₀)) S))
                                    (make-status stack C₊)))
                             ((%Comb? C₀)
                              (let* ((cmds (list (%Comb-rand C₀) (%Comb-rator C₀) sym/apply))
                                     (control (append cmds C₊)))
                                (make-status S control)))
                             (else
                              (error '→/interpreted "not a combination" C₀)))))))))))
  (define-record %Load selector)
  (define-record %Apply)
  (define Load (λ (selector) (make-%Load (%check 'Load procedure? selector))))
  (define Load? %Load?)
  (define Load-selector %Load-selector)
  (define Apply make-%Apply)
  (define Apply? %Apply?)
  (define instruction? (λ (i) (or (%Load? i) (%Apply? i))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Load
    (λ (i out) (format out "(Load ~a)" ((%Load-selector i) (gensym)))))
  (set-record-printer! %Apply (λ (i out) (format out "Apply")))
  (define compile
    (λ (E)
       (rec C
            (λ (c)
               (cond ((%Id? c) (list (Load (K (E (%Id-identifier c))))))
                     ((%Comb? c)
                      (append
                        (C (%Comb-rand c))
                        (C (%Comb-rator c))
                        (list (Apply))))
                     (else (error 'compile "not a combination" c)))))))
  (define →/compiled
    (λ (E)
       (dbind/status
         (λ (s S C)
            (cond ((null? C) s)
                  (else
                   (letcar&cdr
                     (((C₀ C₊) C))
                     (cond ((%Load? C₀)
                            (let1 (stack (cons ((%Load-selector C₀) E) S))
                                  (make-status stack C₊)))
                           ((%Apply? C₀)
                            (match1/first
                              ((,f ,y unquote S₊) S)
                              (make-status (cons (f y) S₊) C₊)))
                           (else
                            (error '→/compiled "not an instruction" C₀)))))))))))

(define (test/value _)

  (⊦= 4
      ((value ((extend E₂) `(add1 unquote add1))) (Comb (Id 'add1) (Id 'a)))))

test/curryfy: pass

(define (test/curryfy _)
  (⊦= (Comb (Comb (Id 'p)
                  (Comb (Comb (Id 'm) (Comb (Comb (Id 'p) (Id 'a)) (Id 'b)))
                        (Id 'c)))
            (Comb (Comb (Id 'f) (Id 'a)) (Id 'c)))
      (curryfy '(p (m (p a b) c) (f a c))))
  (⊦= (Comb (Comb (Id 'p) (Comb (Comb (Id 'f) (Id 'a)) (Id 'c)))
            (Comb (Comb (Id 'm) (Id 'c))
                  (Comb (Comb (Id 'p) (Id 'b)) (Id 'a))))
      (curryfy '(p (f a c) (m c (p b a)))))
  (⊦= (Comb (Comb (Id 'p) (Id 'a)) (Id 'b)) (curryfy '((p a) b)))
  (⊦= (curryfy '(p a b)) (curryfy '((p a) b)))
  (⊦= (Comb (Comb (Comb (Id 'm) (Id 'c)) (Comb (Id 'f) (Id 'a))) (Id 'b))
      (curryfy '((m c (f a)) b))))

test/printer: pass

(define (test/printer _)
  (⊦= "((p a) b)" (with-output-to-string (τ (display (curryfy '(p a b))))))
  (⊦= "((p ((f a) c)) ((m c) ((p b) a)))"
      (with-output-to-string
        (τ (display (curryfy '(p (f a c) (m c (p b a)))))))))

(define (test/traces _)
  (let* ((p (λ (a) (λ (b) (+ a b))))
         (m (λ (a) (λ (b) (- a b))))

         (control (curryfy '(p (m (p a b) c) (f a c))))
         (control₁ (curryfy '(p (f a c) (m c (p b a)))))
         (E ((extend E₀)
             `(a . 1)
             `(b . 2)
             `(c . 3)
             `(p unquote p)
             `(m unquote m)
             `(f unquote f)))
         (→/interpreted (rtc (→/interpreted E)))
         (→/compiled (rtc (→/compiled E)))
         (F (λ (s) (format #t "~a\n" s))))
    (⊦=/normalised
      "(() (((p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) ((f a) c))))\n(() (((f a) c) (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n(() (c (f a) apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((3) ((f a) apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((3) (a f apply0 apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((1 3) (f apply0 apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((#<procedure (f a216)> 1 3) (apply0 apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((#<procedure (f_671 b217)> 3) (apply0 (p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((10) ((p ((m ((p a) b)) c)) apply0))\n((10) (((m ((p a) b)) c) p apply0 apply0))\n((10) (c (m ((p a) b)) apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((3 10) ((m ((p a) b)) apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((3 10) (((p a) b) m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((3 10) (b (p a) apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((2 3 10) ((p a) apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((2 3 10) (a p apply0 apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((1 2 3 10) (p apply0 apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (p a210)> 1 2 3 10) (apply0 apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (f_657 b211)> 2 3 10) (apply0 m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((3 3 10) (m apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (m a213)> 3 3 10) (apply0 apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (f_664 b214)> 3 10) (apply0 p apply0 apply0))\n((0 10) (p apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (p a210)> 0 10) (apply0 apply0))\n((#<procedure (f_657 b211)> 10) (apply0))\n((10) ())\n"

a210)>) Apply Apply))\n((3) ((Load 1) (Load #<procedure (f a216)>) Apply Apply (Load 3) (Load 2) (Load 1) (Load #<procedure (p a210)>) Apply Apply (Load #<procedure (m a213)>) Apply Apply (Load       (⊦= 26 (length t₁))

(aux machine secd): the SECD machine

The SECD machine

Ported from on-scheme's SECD-machine.scm.

Implementation

(module
  (aux machine secd)
  *
  (import
    scheme
    (chicken base)
    (chicken format)
    (chicken string)
    (only srfi-1 list-index last drop-right)
    (aux base)
    (aux commons)
    (aux machine env))
  (define %check
    (λ (who pred? v)
       (cond ((pred? v) v) (else (error who "wrong field type" v)))))
  (define-record %Id identifier)
  (define-record %Lambda var body)
  (define-record %Comb rator rand)
  (define-record %If question answer otherwise)
  (define-record %J)
  (define expression?
    (λ (e) (or (%Id? e) (%Lambda? e) (%Comb? e) (%If? e) (%J? e))))
  (define Id (λ (identifier) (make-%Id (%check 'Id symbol? identifier))))
  (define Lambda
    (λ (var body)
       (make-%Lambda
         (%check 'Lambda symbol? var)
         (%check 'Lambda expression? body))))
  (define Comb
    (λ (rator rand)
       (make-%Comb
         (%check 'Comb expression? rator)
         (%check 'Comb expression? rand))))
  (define If
    (λ (q a o)
       (make-%If
         (%check 'If expression? q)
         (%check 'If expression? a)
         (%check 'If expression? o))))
  (define J make-%J)
  (define-many (Id? Lambda? Comb? If? J?) (%Id? %Lambda? %Comb? %If? %J?))
  (set-record-printer! %Id (λ (e out) (format out "~a" (%Id-identifier e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Lambda
    (λ (e out) (format out "(λ (~a) ~a)" (%Lambda-var e) (%Lambda-body e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Comb
    (λ (e out) (format out "(~a ~a)" (%Comb-rator e) (%Comb-rand e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %If
    (λ (e out)
       (format
         out
         "(if ~a ~a ~a)"
         (%If-question e)
         (%If-answer e)
         (%If-otherwise e))))
  (set-record-printer! %J (λ (e out) (format out "J")))
  (define curryfy
    (λ (sexp)
       (let ((is-Y? (=to? 'Y)) (is-J? (=to? 'J)))
         (cond ((is-J? sexp) (J))
               ((is-Y? sexp)
                (let* ((f (gensym))
                       (h `(λ (g) (,f (λ (x) ((g g) x)))))
                       (Y `(λ (,f) (,h ,h))))
                  (curryfy Y)))
               ((symbol? sexp) (Id sexp))
               ((list? sexp)
                (match/first
                  sexp
                  ((cond (,question ,answer) (else ,otherwise))
                   (If (curryfy question)
                       (curryfy answer)
                       (curryfy otherwise)))
                  ((cond (,question ,answer) unquote clauses)
                   (If (curryfy question)
                       (curryfy answer)
                       (curryfy `(cond ,@clauses))))
                  ((λ (,x) ,body) (Lambda x (curryfy body)))
                  (((λ (,x unquote ys) ,body) ⊣ (pair? ys))
                   (Lambda x (curryfy `(λ ,ys ,body))))
                  ((λ . _) (error "match error for" sexp))
                  ((,rator ,rand) (Comb (curryfy rator) (curryfy rand)))
                  (((,rator ,rand unquote rands) ⊣ (pair? rands))
                   (Comb (curryfy (drop-right sexp 1)) (curryfy (last sexp))))
                  (else (error "match error for" sexp))))
               (else (error "cond error for:" sexp))))))
  (define value
    (λ (E)
       (λ (e)
          (letrec ((V (λ (e)
                         (cond ((%Id? e) (E (%Id-identifier e)))
                               ((%Lambda? e)
                                (let ((var (%Lambda-var e)) (body (%Lambda-body e)))
                                  (λ (x)
                                     (let1 (E₁ ((extend E) `(,var unquote x)))
                                           ((value E₁) body)))))
                               ((%Comb? e)
                                ((V (%Comb-rator e)) (V (%Comb-rand e))))
                               ((%If? e)
                                (if (V (%If-question e))
                                  (V (%If-answer e))
                                  (V (%If-otherwise e))))
                               ((%J? e) (error "((value E) J) not implemented"))
                               (else (error 'value "not an expression" e))))))
            (V e)))))
  (define-record status S E C D)
  (define status-init (λ (E C) (make-status '() E C (void))))
  (define dbind/status
    (λ (recv)
       (λ (s) (recv s (status-S s) (status-E s) (status-C s) (status-D s)))))
  (define status-printer
    (λ (indent)
       (dbind/status
         (λ (s S E C D)
            (let* ((indents (make-string indent #\space))
                   (L (fmap (λ (e)
                               (string-append
                                 (make-string (+ indent 4) #\space)
                                 (to-string e)))))
                   (P (λ (sym l #!key (indents indents))
                         (match/first
                           l
                           (() (format #f "~a(~a ~a)" indents sym '()))
                           ((,l₀) (format #f "~a(~a (~a))" indents sym l₀))
                           ((,l₀ unquote l₊)
                            (let1 (rest (string-intersperse (L l₊) "\n"))
                                  (format #f "~a(~a (~a\n~a))" indents sym l₀ rest))))))
                   (Ss (P "S" S #:indents ""))
                   (Es (P "E" (if (list? E) E (E->alist E))))
                   (Cs (P "C" C))
                   (Ds (cond/λ
                         D
                         (void? (K (format #f "~a(D ~a)" indents D)))
                         (else (K (format
                                    #f
                                    "~a(~a)"
                                    indents
                                    ((status-printer (add1 indent)) D)))))))
              (format #f "~a\n~a\n~a\n~a" Ss Es Cs Ds))))))
  (set-record-printer!
    status
    (λ (s out) (format out "~a" ((status-printer 0) s))))
  (define pop-dump
    (λ (S D)
       ((dbind/status
          (λ (_ S₁ E₁ C₁ D₁) (make-status (cons (car S) S₁) E₁ C₁ D₁)))
        D)))
  (define-record closure C₁ var E)
  (define dbind/closure
    (λ (recv) (λ (c) (recv c (closure-C₁ c) (closure-var c) (closure-E c)))))
  (set-record-printer!
    closure
    (λ (c out)
       (let1 (P (dbind/closure
                  (λ (_ C₁ var E) (format out "[~a ~a ~a]" C₁ var E))))
             (P c))))
  (define →/interpreted
    (let* ((sym/apply (gensym 'apply)) (is-apply? (=to? sym/apply)))
      (dbind/status
        (λ (s S E C D)
           (cond ((and (null? C) (void? D)) s)
                 ((null? C) (pop-dump S D))
                 (else
                  (match1/first
                    ((,C₀ unquote C₊) C)
                    (cond ((is-apply? C₀)
                           (match1/first
                             ((,f ,y unquote S₊) S)
                             (cond/λ
                               f
                               (closure?
                                 (dbind/closure
                                   (λ (_ C₁ j E₁)
                                      (let ((S₂ '())
                                            (E₂ ((extend E₁) `(,j unquote y)))
                                            (C₂ (list C₁))
                                            (D₂ (make-status S₊ E C₊ D)))
                                        (make-status S₂ E₂ C₂ D₂)))))
                               (else (K (make-status (cons (f y) S₊) E C₊ D))))))
                          ((%Id? C₀)
                           (let1 (S₁ (cons (E (%Id-identifier C₀)) S))
                                 (make-status S₁ E C₊ D)))
                          ((%Lambda? C₀)
                           (let1 (c (make-closure
                                      (%Lambda-body C₀)
                                      (%Lambda-var C₀)
                                      E))
                                 (make-status (cons c S) E C₊ D)))
                          ((%Comb? C₀)
                           (let* ((cmds (list (%Comb-rand C₀) (%Comb-rator C₀) sym/apply))
                                  (C₁ (append cmds C₊)))
                             (make-status S E C₁ D)))
                          (else
                           (error '→/interpreted "no transition for" C₀))))))))))
  (define-record %Id₋ id)
  (define-record %Id₊ index)
  (define-record %Lambda₊ body)
  (define-record %Comb₊ rator rand)
  (define-record %If₊ question answer otherwise)
  (define-record %J₊)
  (define de-bruijn?
    (λ (e)
       (or (%Id₋? e) (%Id₊? e) (%Lambda₊? e) (%Comb₊? e) (%If₊? e) (%J₊? e))))
  (define Id₋ (λ (id) (make-%Id₋ (%check 'Id₋ symbol? id))))
  (define Id₊ (λ (index) (make-%Id₊ (%check 'Id₊ number? index))))
  (define Lambda₊ (λ (body) (make-%Lambda₊ (%check 'Lambda₊ de-bruijn? body))))
  (define Comb₊
    (λ (rator rand)
       (make-%Comb₊
         (%check 'Comb₊ de-bruijn? rator)
         (%check 'Comb₊ de-bruijn? rand))))
  (define If₊
    (λ (q a o)
       (make-%If₊
         (%check 'If₊ de-bruijn? q)
         (%check 'If₊ de-bruijn? a)
         (%check 'If₊ de-bruijn? o))))
  (define J₊ make-%J₊)
  (define-many
    (Id₋? Id₊? Lambda₊? Comb₊? If₊? J₊?)
    (%Id₋? %Id₊? %Lambda₊? %Comb₊? %If₊? %J₊?))
  (set-record-printer! %Id₋ (λ (e out) (format out "~a" (%Id₋-id e))))
  (set-record-printer! %Id₊ (λ (e out) (format out "~a" (%Id₊-index e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Lambda₊
    (λ (e out) (format out "(λ ~a)" (%Lambda₊-body e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Comb₊
    (λ (e out) (format out "(~a ~a)" (%Comb₊-rator e) (%Comb₊-rand e))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %If₊
    (λ (e out)
       (format
         out
         "(if ~a ~a ~a)"
         (%If₊-question e)
         (%If₊-answer e)
         (%If₊-otherwise e))))
  (set-record-printer! %J₊ (λ (e out) (format out "J")))
  (define expression->de-bruijn
    (λ (e)
       (letrec ((deB (λ (e F)
                        (let1 (position (λ (v) (list-index (=to? v) F)))
                              (cond ((%Id? e)
                                     (let1 (i (%Id-identifier e))
                                           (cond/λ
                                             (position i)
                                             (number? (λ (p) (Id₊ p)))
                                             (else (K (Id₋ i))))))
                                    ((%Lambda? e)
                                     (Lambda₊
                                       (deB (%Lambda-body e) (cons (%Lambda-var e) F))))
                                    ((%Comb? e)
                                     (Comb₊ (deB (%Comb-rator e) F)
                                            (deB (%Comb-rand e) F)))
                                    ((%If? e)
                                     (If₊ (deB (%If-question e) F)
                                          (deB (%If-answer e) F)
                                          (deB (%If-otherwise e) F)))
                                    ((%J? e) (J₊))
                                    (else
                                     (error 'expression->de-bruijn
                                            "not an expression"
                                            e)))))))
         (deB e '()))))
  (define value₊
    (λ (E)
       (λ (e)
          (letrec ((deB (λ (e F)
                           (cond ((%Id₋? e) (E (%Id₋-id e)))
                                 ((%Id₊? e) (list-ref F (%Id₊-index e)))
                                 ((%Lambda₊? e)
                                  (λ (y) (deB (%Lambda₊-body e) (cons y F))))
                                 ((%Comb₊? e)
                                  ((deB (%Comb₊-rator e) F)
                                   (deB (%Comb₊-rand e) F)))
                                 ((%If₊? e)
                                  (if (deB (%If₊-question e) F)
                                    (deB (%If₊-answer e) F)
                                    (deB (%If₊-otherwise e) F)))
                                 ((%J₊? e)
                                  (error "((value₊ E) J) not implemented"))
                                 (else (error 'value₊ "not a de Bruijn term" e))))))
            (deB e '())))))
  (define-record %Load selector)
  (define-record %Apply)
  (define-record %Position index)
  (define-record %Position&Apply index)
  (define-record %Closure control)
  (define-record %Enter)
  (define-record %Exit)
  (define-record %Test answer otherwise)
  (define-record %Jump)
  (define instruction?
    (λ (i)
       (or (%Load? i)
           (%Apply? i)
           (%Position? i)
           (%Position&Apply? i)
           (%Closure? i)
           (%Enter? i)
           (%Exit? i)
           (%Test? i)
           (%Jump? i))))
  (define Load (λ (selector) (make-%Load (%check 'Load procedure? selector))))
  (define Apply make-%Apply)
  (define Position
    (λ (index) (make-%Position (%check 'Position number? index))))
  (define Position&Apply
    (λ (index) (make-%Position&Apply (%check 'Position&Apply number? index))))
  (define Closure
    (λ (control) (make-%Closure (%check 'Closure list? control))))
  (define Enter make-%Enter)
  (define Exit make-%Exit)
  (define Test
    (λ (answer otherwise)
       (make-%Test
         (%check 'Test list? answer)
         (%check 'Test list? otherwise))))
  (define Jump make-%Jump)
  (define-many
    (Load? Apply? Position? Position&Apply? Closure? Enter? Exit? Test? Jump?)
    (%Load?
      %Apply?
      %Position?
      %Position&Apply?
      %Closure?
      %Enter?
      %Exit?
      %Test?
      %Jump?))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Load
    (λ (i out) (format out "(Load ~a)" ((%Load-selector i) identity))))
  (set-record-printer! %Apply (λ (i out) (format out "Apply")))
  (set-record-printer! %Enter (λ (i out) (format out "Enter")))
  (set-record-printer! %Exit (λ (i out) (format out "Exit")))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Test
    (λ (i out)
       (format out "(Test ~a ~a)" (%Test-answer i) (%Test-otherwise i))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Position
    (λ (i out) (format out "(Position ~a)" (%Position-index i))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Position&Apply
    (λ (i out) (format out "(Position&Apply ~a)" (%Position&Apply-index i))))
  (set-record-printer!
    %Closure
    (λ (i out) (format out "(Closure ~a)" (%Closure-control i))))
  (set-record-printer! %Jump (λ (i out) (format out "Jump")))
  (define compiler
    (λ (who comb)
       (rec C
            (λ (e)
               (cond ((%Id₋? e) (list (Load ($ (%Id₋-id e)))))
                     ((%Id₊? e) (list (Position (%Id₊-index e))))
                     ((%Lambda₊? e) (list (Closure (C (%Lambda₊-body e)))))
                     ((%Comb₊? e) (comb C (%Comb₊-rator e) (%Comb₊-rand e)))
                     ((%If₊? e)
                      `(,@(C (%If₊-question e))
                        ,(Test (C (%If₊-answer e)) (C (%If₊-otherwise e)))))
                     ((%J₊? e) (list (Jump)))
                     (else (error who "not a de Bruijn term" e)))))))
  (define comb/generic (λ (C rator rand) `(,@(C rand) ,@(C rator) ,(Apply))))
  (define compile (compiler 'compile comb/generic))
  (define-record closure₊ C E)
  (define dbind/closure₊
    (λ (recv) (λ (c) (recv c (closure₊-C c) (closure₊-E c)))))
  (set-record-printer!
    closure₊
    (λ (c out) (format out "[~a ~a]" (closure₊-C c) (closure₊-E c))))
  (define-record program-closure body D)
  (define dbind/program-closure
    (λ (recv)
       (λ (pc) (recv pc (program-closure-body pc) (program-closure-D pc)))))
  (set-record-printer!
    program-closure
    (λ (pc out)
       (format
         out
         "<[~a ~a]>"
         (program-closure-body pc)
         (program-closure-D pc))))
  (define →/compiled/rules
    (λ (PA other)
       (λ (E₀)
          (dbind/status
            (λ (s S E C D)
               (cond ((and (null? C) (void? D)) s)
                     ((null? C) (pop-dump S D))
                     (else
                      (match1/first
                        ((,C₀ unquote C₊) C)
                        (cond ((%Load? C₀)
                               (let1 (S₁ (cons ((%Load-selector C₀) E₀) S))
                                     (make-status S₁ E C₊ D)))
                              ((%Position? C₀)
                               (let1 (S₁ (cons (list-ref E (%Position-index C₀)) S))
                                     (make-status S₁ E C₊ D)))
                              ((%Closure? C₀)
                               (let1 (c₊ (make-closure₊ (%Closure-control C₀) E))
                                     (make-status (cons c₊ S) E C₊ D)))
                              ((%Test? C₀)
                               (match1/first
                                 ((,boolean unquote S₊) S)
                                 (let1 (branch
                                         (if boolean
                                           (%Test-answer C₀)
                                           (%Test-otherwise C₀)))
                                       (make-status S₊ E (append branch C₊) D))))
                              ((%Apply? C₀)
                               (match1/first
                                 ((,f ,y unquote S₊) S)
                                 (PA f y S₊ E C₊ D)))
                              (else (other C₀ S E C₊ D)))))))))))
  (define PA/compiled
    (λ (f y S E C D)
       (cond/λ
         f
         (closure₊?
           (dbind/closure₊
             (λ (_ C₁ E₁)
                (let ((S₂ '())
                      (E₂ (cons y E₁))
                      (C₂ (identity C₁))
                      (D₂ (make-status S E C D)))
                  (make-status S₂ E₂ C₂ D₂)))))
         (else (K (make-status (cons (f y) S) E C D))))))
  (define →/compiled
    (→/compiled/rules
      PA/compiled
      (λ (C₀ S E C D) (error '→/compiled "no transition for" C₀))))
  (define compile⁺
    (compiler
      'compile⁺
      (λ (C rator rand)
         (cond ((%Lambda₊? rator)
                `(,@(C rand) ,(Enter) ,@(C (%Lambda₊-body rator)) ,(Exit)))
               ((%Id₊? rator)
                `(,@(C rand) ,(Position&Apply (%Id₊-index rator))))
               (else (comb/generic C rator rand))))))
  (define-record status-appender status)
  (define dbind/status-appender
    (λ (recv) (λ (sa) (recv sa (status-appender-status sa)))))
  (set-record-printer!
    status-appender
    (λ (sa out) (format out "<|~a|>" (status-appender-status sa))))
  (define →/compiled⁺
    (let ((PA (λ (f y S E C D)
                 (cond/λ
                   f
                   (program-closure?
                     (dbind/program-closure
                       (λ (_ body D₀)
                          (cond/λ
                            D₀
                            (void? (K (let ((S₁ `(,body ,y)) (C₁ (cons (Apply) '())))
                                        (make-status S₁ E C₁ D₀))))
                            (status?
                              (dbind/status
                                (λ (_ S₁ E₁ C₁ D₁)
                                   (let ((S₂ `(,body ,y unquote S₁))
                                         (E₂ (identity E₁))
                                         (C₂ (cons (Apply) C₁))
                                         (D₂ (identity D₁)))
                                     (make-status S₂ E₂ C₂ D₂)))))
                            (else error)))))
                   (status-appender?
                     (dbind/status-appender
                       (λ (_ D₀)
                          (let1 (pc (make-program-closure y D₀))
                                (make-status (cons pc S) E C D)))))
                   (else (λ (f) (PA/compiled f y S E C D)))))))
      (→/compiled/rules
        PA
        (λ (C₀ S E C₊ D)
           (cond ((%Enter? C₀)
                  (match1/first
                    ((,s₀ unquote S₊) S)
                    (let1 (D₁ (make-status S₊ '() '() D))
                          (make-status '() (cons s₀ E) C₊ D₁))))
                 ((%Exit? C₀)
                  (let ((S₁ (cons (car S) (status-S D)))
                        (E₁ (cdr E))
                        (C₁ C₊)
                        (D₁ (status-D D)))
                    (make-status S₁ E₁ C₁ D₁)))
                 ((%Position&Apply? C₀)
                  (let1 (f (list-ref E (%Position&Apply-index C₀)))
                        (match1/first ((,y unquote S₊) S) (PA f y S₊ E C₊ D))))
                 ((%Jump? C₀)
                  (let1 (S₁ (cons (make-status-appender D) S))
                        (make-status S₁ E C₊ D)))
                 (else (error '→/compiled⁺ "not an instruction" C₀))))))))

test/de-bruijn: pass

(define (test/de-bruijn _)
  (let1 (t '(λ (x) ((g x) (λ (y) ((λ (z) (x y z)) (f x))))))
        (⊦= "(λ ((g 0) (λ ((λ ((2 1) 0)) (f 1)))))"
            ((○ to-string expression->de-bruijn curryfy) t)))
  (⊦= "(λ (f) ((λ (g) (f (λ (x) ((g g) x)))) (λ (g) (f (λ (x) ((g g) x))))))"
      (to-string Y₀))
  (⊦= "(λ ((λ (1 (λ ((1 1) 0)))) (λ (1 (λ ((1 1) 0))))))"
      ((○ to-string expression->de-bruijn curryfy) 'Y)))

test/compiled-rules: pass

(define (test/compiled-rules _)
  (let1 (no-rule
          (λ (→ C)
             (condition-case
               (begin ((→ (make-E)) (status-init '() C)) #f)
               (e (exn) ((condition-property-accessor 'exn 'message) e)))))
        (for-each
          (λ (i) (⊦= "no transition for" (no-rule →/compiled (list i))))
          (list (Enter) (Exit) (Position&Apply 0) (Jump)))
        (⊦= "not an instruction" (no-rule →/compiled⁺ (list 'Load))))
  (⊦= (map to-string (→/compiled* s₁)) (map to-string (→/compiled⁺* s₁))))

test/if-non-tail: pass

(define (test/if-non-tail _)
  (let* ((E ((extend E₀)
             `(add1 unquote add1)
             '(one . 1)
             '(two . 2)
             '(three . 3)
             '(t . #t)
             '(f . #f)
             `(+ unquote (λ (x) (λ (y) (+ x y))))))
         (run (λ (comp →)
                 (λ (sexp)
                    (let1 (t ((○ comp expression->de-bruijn curryfy) sexp))
                          ((○ car status-S last (rtc (→ E)))
                           (status-init '() t)))))))
    (for-each
      (λ (sexp)
         (let1 (v ((○ (value E) curryfy) sexp))
               (⊦= v ((run compile →/compiled) sexp))
               (⊦= v ((run compile⁺ →/compiled⁺) sexp))))
      '((add1 (cond (f one) (else two)))
        (add1 (cond (t one) (else two)))
        (add1 ((λ (x) (cond (x two) (else three))) f))
        ((λ (x) (cond (x one) (else two))) f)
        (+ (cond (f one) (t two) (else three)) (cond (t one) (else two)))))
    (⊦= "((Load f) (Test ((Load one)) ((Load two))) (Load add1) Apply)"
        ((○ to-string compile expression->de-bruijn curryfy)
         '(add1 (cond (f one) (else two)))))))

((eta 0.0) (memory #(12582912 2895600 1048576)) (stdout "") (stderr ""))

test/curryfy-clauses: pass

(define (test/curryfy-clauses _)
  (⊦= "(((f a) b) c)" ((○ to-string curryfy) '(f a b c)))
  (⊦= "(λ (x) (λ (y) (λ (z) x)))" ((○ to-string curryfy) '(λ (x y z) x)))
  (⊦= "(if p a b)" ((○ to-string curryfy) '(cond (p a) (else b))))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(cond (p a))))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(f)))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(λ (x) a b)))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(λ (x y) a b)))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(λ () a)))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '(λ (x))))
  (⊨/error (curryfy '()))
  (⊨/error (curryfy 3))
  (⊨/error (Lambda "x" (Id 'y)))
  (⊨/error ((value₊ E₀) (J₊))))
